## Release 2.8.0 (Sep 19 09:45 +02 / 07:45 UTC Sat Sep 19): **TRADING ANALYTICS & REVIEW — new dashboard section + A4 reports (deployed, Joachim-requested feature)** - release 2.8.0 (bundle md5 **2664cfec8bf746829f14d3f5d0f6eec5**, 357,050 B, RELEASE_VERSION 2.8.0), 261 tests / 956 assertions PASS (18 new analytics tests), typecheck 0. **New:** (1) `src/analytics.ts` — deterministic Trading-Analytics engine computing per-closed-trade analysis from stored D1 data only: Decision Quality scored from ENTRY-TIME information exclusively (TA snapshot emaTrend/macdTrend/adx/volumeRatio/rsi/pricePosition/vwapDeviation + confidence + planned R:R from intent stop/target; realized outcome NEVER enters the score — no hindsight/outcome bias; labels Strong/Good/Questionable/Weak + internal 0-100 score), Execution Quality (slippage = |fill - price_at_decision| vs ATR tolerance), Trade Management (planned-envelope adherence proxy), Exit Quality (close_reason semantics), Outcome (P/L, P/L %, realized R from stored stop × qty, initial risk in USD), acceptable-vs-avoidable loss classification (Strong/Good + compliant + slippage-within-ATR = acceptable), whatWorked/whatCouldImprove/learningPoint generated from concrete findings only, MFE/MAE/funding/leverage explicitly null ("Insufficient data" in UI) because the broker integration does not record them — NOTHING fabricated. (2) `GET /api/analytics` — strictly read-only, period (7/30/90/YTD/all/custom), filters (strategy, symbol, side, result, regime, confidence band); returns KPIs (net P/L, win rate, profit factor, expectancy + expectancy-in-R, avg win/loss, avg planned R:R, max drawdown, avg duration, attributed fees, long/short split, avg decision quality, avg realized R), cumulative P/L + drawdown curves, confidence calibration (5 bands) with explicit miscalibration verdict, combination pattern analysis (side × regime × volatility × confidence-band, n>=3, evidence Preliminary/Moderate/Strong by sample size, chronological 60/40 out-of-sample check), winner/loser top/bottom 20%, day-vs-swing comparison across risk/consistency/expectancy/drawdown/costs/duration/result-volatility (no simplistic "best" verdict), research backlog (Title/Observation/Evidence/Sample size/Hypothesis/Proposed test/Success criteria/Status=New — hypotheses to backtest, never direct strategy changes), robustness block with chronological split. (3) Dashboard "Analytics" tab — same design tokens/components, period buttons + custom dates, filters, KPI grid, cumulative P/L + drawdown charts, calibration table, patterns (What Works / Needs Attention), day-vs-swing comparison, research backlog, trade-by-trade analysis with per-trade detail (market context, ORIGINAL AI thesis unchanged, entry/execution/management/exit review, outcome, data gaps). (4) A4 reports — Export Day/Swing Trading Report buttons build a one-page A4 portrait sheet (header, 6-8 KPIs, cumulative P/L main graph, max 3 What Works + 3 Improvements, 3-5 line Trading Review; priority-ordered content so it can NEVER exceed one page; black/white-safe with symbols not color-only) and open the print dialog for Save-as-PDF. (5) `analytics_snapshots` D1 table + daily watermark-gated snapshot write in scheduled maintenance (`last_analytics_snapshot_date` config, ONCE/day, idempotent upsert) so improvement over time is measurable; table also created idempotently by the writer (analytics-snapshots-migration.sql for fresh installs). Original AI thesis (decisions.reason/ai_reasoning) is never rewritten; post-trade review text is a separate field. Caps/guards/min_confidence 0.8 UNTOUCHED by this release. NEXT: verify Analytics tab + /api/analytics live post-deploy; Mon Sep 21 13:30 UTC min-confidence-0.8 verification unchanged (C-726); MFE/MAE capture = candidate follow-up (needs per-bar position tracking). C-727 release entry (this control).

## Control-725 / Release 2.7.1 (Sep 19 09:30 +02 / 07:30 UTC Sat Sep 19): **FINDING 1 FIXED — min_confidence 0.8 now LIVE (deployed, authorized by Joachim's decision delegate Sep 19)** - release 2.7.1 (src bundle md5 **a8b37503**, 321,336 B, RELEASE_VERSION 2.7.1), PUT /content multipart success + PUT /schedules ARRAY re-registration success (all 4 crons modified_on 2026-09-19T07:23:51Z), live /health = **2.7.1** verified. **Root cause (FINDING 1, open since C-491 Sep 9):** `resolveDaytradingConfig` in src/index.ts merged D1 rows with `if (key in config)` where D1 keys are snake_case (`min_confidence`) and FALLBACK_CONFIG keys are camelCase (`minConfidence`) — NO D1 override ever merged, so the approved 0.8 silently lost to the hard-coded 0.7 fallback (live-proven rejections "below minimum 0.7" + approvals at 0.7864/0.725/0.757). **Fix:** snake→camel normalization (`key.replace(/_([a-z])/g, ...)`) before the membership check; audited ALL D1 keys against FALLBACK_CONFIG — min_confidence 0.8 is the ONLY value that changes behavior (every other D1 value equals its fallback default: max_positions 15, stop_loss_pct 8, eod_flatten true, llm_model glm-5p2, etc.); caps stay 5000/3700/2000 (max_capital_usd handled separately by resolveCapitalCapOverride, untouched); swing/crypto configs use their own resolvers, untouched. Joachim delegated the 0.7-vs-0.8 decision Sep 19 ("Du skal beslutte") with the directive "tjene penge, ikke miste penge" — decided **0.8** on evidence: daytrading lifetime realized -$9.32 across 51 closed positions (30% win rate) = the 0.70-0.79 band never made money, and marginal-confidence entries with fees/spread carry negative expectancy. Validation: **243 tests / 903 assertions PASS** (2 new FINDING-1 regression tests: snake_case merge + only-overriding-keys contract), typecheck 0, `git diff --check` clean. Version surfaces ALL aligned 2.7.1 (src/version.ts, package.json, schema.sql seed, dashboard/index.html marker+footer, version-contract test). Post-deploy live verification: /health 2.7.0→**2.7.1** exact, runs 14146→14150 flowing 0 errors 0 lease-holds (cron + reconcile cadencing normally), schedules API confirms all 4 crons registered fresh. ROLLBACK plan: prior 2.7.0 bundle (md5 e34318f5, src 2fe9c5a) rebuildable from git b7db43f and PUT-able by the same multipart method. NEXT: the 0.8 gate fires live at **Mon Sep 21 13:30 UTC open** — rejections will read "below minimum 0.8" and MSTR-class 0.70-0.79 approvals should cease; verify at Monday's first control. Escalation predicate stays ARMED (any new suppression recurrence = ESCALATE); pending Joachim: D1 paid-tier, daytrading cap fill-drift re-check. C-725 release entry (this control).
